Western Division

The Western Division of the Constabulary stretches
from the county boundaries with Dorset and Wiltshire
In the West, across the City of Southampton, and
beyond to the River Hamble in the East, and north-
ward to Include Romsey and Eastleigh.

it has a rich diversity of features including the densely

populated inner city suburbs, major port and ariport as
well as coastlines, rural sections and the New Forest.

Western Divisional Police Headquarters

17 Aug 95

FILE NOTE

REPORT OF AN UNEXPLAINED ABRIAL SIGHTING

1. Received a call from s/ CRO at Honnington.

2. >A called him to report a sighting
at 0030 hrs last night. Slow moving aircraft with landing
lights on, heading from Honnington to Thetford. There was no
discernable noise. Once the aircraft passed him he noticed 2
after-burners and then the whole sight seemed to break up and

disappear.

3. SECO just wanted to report it to someone. No other
details.

DETAILS OF OCCURRENCE

POSSTBLE UFO SIGHTING

On 19 Jul 95 at 2355 hrs, Section 4 RMers in his garden, with his dog.
Whilst he was observing the sky he saw an orange light, similar in
colour and intensity as a sodium light, in the shape of a disc.
The disc was moving from south east to north west, then it appeared to
turn sharply, almost 90 degrees, to the southwest. There was no engine
noise heard and he observed the whole incident for about 30 seconds.
The height was difficult to estimate in the night sky.

explained that he had not been drinking that evening and he
was not a UFO "Buff". He appeared to be shaken by the incident.
His address where the incident occurred is:

St Neots AO |
Cambridgeshire

TITLE OF OCCURRENCE
REPORTS THE SIGHTING OF POSSIBLE UFO

DETAILS OF OCCURRENCE
1. SERIO who claimed to be ex~-RAFPERMREEMIM and also to have
left the RAF in 1984 stated that he had seen what he thought was a UFO
over Poole Harbour at 0415 hours this a.m.

2. He described it as Silver in colour with bright lights and sounded
like a helicopter on the ground( a low, slow beating sound). He gave
no description of height or direction of movement.

3. He had not reported this occurrence to the Civil Police.

FILE NOTE

REPORT OF AN UNEXPLAINED AERIAL SIGHTING

1. The RCRO (Borders), rang to say that a BEG” had
called reporting a sighting to RAF Prestwick. He didn't have
the details so I called her.

2. of East Kilbride, told me
that at 2100 hrs on Friday 23 Jun she thought she heard an
aircraft noise above her house so she went outside to look.

She saw a formation of lights in the sky above her house but
they didnot look like aircraft lights, more like stars. The
night was clear. She witnessed the sighting with her mother,
who rang Glasgow airport, who weren't able to shed any light on
what they may have been.

3. I explained our role and offered to call her if an
explanation was forthcoming to account for the lights.

4, No evidence of a matter of defence concern.

2 Jun 95

FILE NOTE

REPORT OF AN UNEXPLAINED AERIAL SIGHTING

1. Received a call fron SESE of Hull

He saw two large triangular shaped objects flying fast past his
house on Wednesday 31 May at 0015 hrs. There was a bright
light in the middle of them. After a few moments they seemed
to separate and quick as a flash they were gone.

2. He telephoned Leeds ATCC who confirmed that there was no
civil air traffic on their radars and they weren't expecting
anything til 0400 hrs.

3. I explained our interest and role and said if any light
was shed on his sighting then we would let him know.
No evidence of defence concern yet.

In 40
proned

D/Sec(AS) /12/2

2 Jun 95

FILE NOTE

REPORT OF AN UNEXPLAINED AERIAL SIGHTING

1. EOS from Market Bosworth,
Leicestershire, called to report a sighting witnessed at
0300 hrs this morning.

2. He described the object as large and “space ship" shaped.
It was silent and hovered/circling over Market Bosworth for 15
minutes or so before completely disappearing. There were green
lights at the base of the object. He said that 3 of his
workmates (they were all on nightshift) saw it as well.

3. He stressed that he was not a "loony" and I explained our
role, that of course he saw something but that it was a matter
of interpretation. I took his number and said that if anything
came to light which explained the sighting that I would call
him.

Dear Sir,

Re: UFO Sighting - 27th of May 1995

The following report was received at Braintree Police Station at 0949
hours on the 28th of May 1995. —

A. 2300 hours 27/05/95 - 0030 hours 28/05/95
B. 1 object, 200 feet diameter, large green tinted lights on outer

edge, small circle of lights inside that, outer edge revolving.
No sounds mentioned.

c. In open air.
D. Naked eye and camera (still).
E. Following power cables 3 mile west of Wickham Market, Suffolk.
F/G. Seen from % mile away to a height just above power line pylons.
H. Followed power cables.

‘ J. Dark night with cloud cover.
K. High voltage power lines.

L. Police only by informant,

Ministry of Defence & West Prayton A.T.C.C.
by Officer reporting.

FILE NOTE

REPORT OF AN UNEXPLAINED AERIAL SIGHTING

1. HERDED telephoned to discuss a sighting he

witnessed in the early hours of Sunday morning (0102 hrs).

He saw what looked like a shooting star, green in colour and
its trail went from green to orange to yellow. He was near
Northampton, on the A43 on the Towcester to Northampton road.

2. I explained our role. However, earlier in the day we had
received 4 reported sightings from Leuchars, which appeared
similar in description, between 0100 and 0115 hrs on Sunday
morning. Leuchars made a note on the reports that they had
checked with Edinburgh airport, and they had witnessed a

' possible meteor in the Glenrothes to Kirkcaldy area which could
account for the sighting.

3. I told the above to Section 40| tate gave him the phone nos
of the Royal Greenwich Observatory, and the British Fireball

society, in case he wanted to contact them.

4. I took his phone no should any other explanation come to
light

25 May 95
FILE NOTE
REPORT OF AN UNEXPLAINED AERIAL SIGHTING
1. Received two phonecalls of sightings which tied in with

the Fireball over Kirkcaldy/Glenrothes on the early hours of
Sunday morning 21 May (0100 hrs).

2. Section 40S Fort William (didnot leave her details)
saw the same sight as all_those who reported sightings to
Leuchars (5 witnesses). [Section 40| Ra Rothbury

also saw exactly the same thing, although it was his perception
that it was purply in colour.

3. I explained our role to both. All the timings of all the
witnesses tie in with one another.

4. I spoke to the Royal Greenwich Observatory and when I had
jive a © list of descriptions he concluded
that i ad all the hallmarks of a fireball. The green colour

in particular is the heat of the object hitting the earth's
atmosphere and oxydising. ~

16 May 95

FILE NOTE

REPORT OF UNEXPLAINED AERIAL SIGHTING

1. Received a tel call from Pysection 40 fis saw a bright light
in the sky in the evening of 15/5/95. The sighting took place
over Tunbridge Wells.

2. SEE asked if there was any military a/c activity in
the area of Tunbridge Wells at around 21:45. I checked this
with the TBC (Tactical Booking Cell) and they advised that
there was no activity in that area at that time.

3. I Petteg section 40 Fe pass on the info and explained our
role. I said the object he described sounded like a civy plane
on a heading in line with him at the time . I also gave him the
address of BUFORA and a tel number.

SUBJECT: REPORT OF AN UNIDENTIFIED OBJECT

1. At 2355 hours on Thursday 4th May 1995 the following named
persons visited Chasetown Police Station, both in an agitated and
distressed state reporting an alleged encounter with a U.F.O.

Chasetown.

Section40,
They were extremely excited and initially requested me to
and view a U.F.O. which was still visible. Myself and
went to the front of the station. There was a red light
visible in the far distance with white lights surrounding but these
could quite\easily have been a civil airline.

Section 40

3. We returned to the Police Station and both youths attempted
to convey to me what had allegedly happened. They stated that whilst
walking up the Rugeley Road, Burntwood from the direction of the
Nags Head Public House to Swan Island at approximately 2255 hours,
at a point near the Fulfin School, they both experienced an intense

heat. Their skin turned a glowing red. They then stated they saw
a darkish silver inverted saucer shaped object in a field which

lowing red beneath, (brief drawing provided at time by Eh 40
sedi

Appendix A). They stated the object was about four houses
high in the sky and about forty foot away from them, They then,
reluctantly, went on to state that a voice which came from a
lemon like head! which appeared beneath the machine,said, "We
want you, come with us". Both appeared upset and shocked and as
it was increasingly difficult to obtain detailed information from
them they were told to go home and write an account of what they
thought they had experienced for our information.

4, On Sth May 1995 the attached written accounts of the ‘encounter'
were delivered to Chasetown Police Station, Appendix B from

Appendix C from RSais=Wiall

5. On 6th May 1995 the two informants were taken to the area and
the exact location of ‘sighting' pin pointed to P.C.

Enquiries locally reveal no further witnesses but a nearby resident
stated a local farmer was crop spraying in a field at the material
time. The farner , Ea has been spoken to and confirms

Cont'd Overleaf / Sheet No.

“LINN Ltauid ‘O'H B0MOd BuIHSAUOdavES

the crop sprayer being used but stated he did not see any
persons in the field, speak to anyone or see anything
unusual.

6. In addition to aforementioned appendixes attached,
please find information required in format as laid down
by Force Standing Order, E.1.1. I ask that this report
together with appendiges be forwarded to, Department of

tat Trade and Industry, National Air Traffic Control Services,
1, Victoria Street, London SW1H OET.

FILE NOTE

|, EES sonersct REM (no

telephone) called.

2. At 1300 hrs today just above Wellington/Black Down Hills
saw an airliner going overhead, and just to one side of the
aircraft was a shining object which was following the aircraft.
It remained close to the aircraft for a minute, then it shot
away to the right then came to a dead standstill. for another
30 seconds.

3. He said this may be a coincidence, but a few moments later
some American F-15 aircraft shot across the sky as if in search
of the craft. He was adamant that it was not another aircraft.

4. He wanted to report the incident, (a) because he felt he
ought to and he wanted to know if anyone else had seen it, but
(b) he wanted to do it straight away as it would diminish his
eredibility if he left it a few days and the incident was
reported on the radio or in the Press, and he would then look
like someone jumping on the bandwagon.

5. I explained our interest and said if an explanation was
forthcoming I would let him know. Alternatively, I said that
if anyone else reported the same incident I would let him know
(obviously protecting the witness' confidentiality).

Station: Saffron Walden

officer: rc EEO

Subject: Unidentified Flying Object

Date: 3 April 1995 Ref: S.O. 36.81

AST
PSL4173

I submit this report in accordance with Force Standing Orders, Section
36.81 in respect of Unidentified flying objects.

(A). Date/Time, duration of sighting:

Between 0001 and 0040 on Saturday, 1 April, 1995 for a
duration of 39 minutes,

(B). Description:

The object was circular in shape with an outer area made
up of a series of miss-shapes,; the inner area being made
up by the same miss-shapes. (See attached drawing). The
object was between twenty to thirty feet across, emitted
no sound or smell, it was pale in colour with cream and
yellow lights. (The pattern or make up of the object was
circular to that of an old fashioned typed parafin heater).

(C). Position of observers:

The object was observed from half way down the front path
of Section 40 a Wimbish, the object was about fifty foot
from the observers (outdoors).

(D). How observed:
By the naked eye.

(BE). Direction etc.

The object was directly above the roof Prysection 40,

(two houses down). However, the object was first
observed by the son and his friend in Saffron Walden at

0001 a.m, 1 April, 1995, in the skies above an followed
(F). Angle of sight:

About 450
(G). Distance:

Difficult to give an exact distance, but about fifty feet away.
op :870 *

(H). Movements:
Remained static above number 14 for some time.

(J). Meteorological conditions:

Low cloud, overcast.

(K). Nearby object:

Tye Green has both overhead power (electric) and phone
cables to the houses.

(L). To whom reported:

Essex Police, Saffron Walden.

(M). Informant:

(N). Any Background: -
[Section 40

The object was seen by both ES con and his friend
in Saffron Walden and the object followed them to

where it remained, the two young men having watched the
object for some time, called for Section 40, join them,
which she did.

(0). ZOE (son of informant), SNE and

{P). Incident Number 0759 of 2 April, 1995 at 1607 hours.
(Copy attached).

In accordance with Force Standing Orders, I request that this report,
along with any relevant documentation is forwarded to:-

Ministry of Defence,
Air Force Operations
Main Building Room Stim 40

Whitehall, london, SW1A 2HB

FOEILE REPORT

1. Section 40, Havafod West, Fishguard called. She saw a
very bright light like a star hoovering for about half an hour
in the sky. It was a long way away.

2. This one sounded to me very much like a headlight from a
Jumbo which was miles and miles away coming toward her on a
straight line, which gave the appearance that the light just
didnot move.

3. I advised BERMMIRM of our line and took a telephone no to
let her know if something came to light to explain the
sighting.

2. There was no sound and the light was bulb shaped. Section 4d]
works for the MAFF. She appeared interested in pursuing this

3. BERR explained our role and said if any information on

PILE NOTE

1. Today, i” telephoned to say
that on Wed I5 March at 2030 hrs he saw a yellow object,

similar to the moon shoot across the sky and disintegrate. BEMign 40)

9 Mar 95
UFO REPORTED SIGHTINGS

I received two telephone calls this afternoon reporting two
unexplained sightings:

1.
Sighting Wed 8 Mar - 1915 hours he saw a light in the distance

and a craft which he could only describe as yo-yo shaped. He
was on the A711 between Dalbeattie and Dumfrees. The object
has red and green lights.

2. EE 2otherhan,

S Yorkshire. He was on the Ml northbound, coming off at J36.
He saw a craft hovering, it was bright electric blue in colour,
but he ended up convincing himself that it was probably a

balloon, once I explained that SSSA) 2 s balloon with lights
inside. He wanted to take the details of BUFORA and Quest so I

gave him their numbers.

For both instances, having explained our role and interest,
I advised that if any information came to light from other
sources, I would let them know.

7 Mar 95
COUDHURST, KENT - OE (ier rfum's)
1. has seen through her

skylight for the last 3 nights (at about 0230 hrs) 2 bright
pink lights with a pink surrounding glow. They do not move.
Her husband thinks they may be a satellite.

2. I explained our role, and took her tele no and said if
anything came to light I would let her know.

1. Yesterday evening between 1930 and 2000 her daughter ran
in from one of their fields and said she saw a round ball
object with a green and red flashing light hovering about house
high in front of her. The daughter is 11 years old and she
later drew what she saw. ;

2. I explained our role, and offered my thoughts that it was
likely to be a civil helicopter hovering over the field. That
would fit in with the ball shape and the nav lights. Section ila]
Section Agr eae! her daughter said there was no sound.

3. Again I took her number and if anything comes to light
said I would call her.

REPORT OF UNEXPLAINED AERIAL PHENOMENA

Witness:

St Albans Area

1340 hrs today whilst driving in her car with her young son

they spied a cigar shaped object in the sky floating

like ....(she had to stop herself from saying “like a

balloon"). It had a bright light on the end and she was

convinced it was not a balloon - she had seen the Section 4fgj
before and this was a lot higher in the sky.

I explained our interest, and said that if anything came to
light I would let her know to solve her curiosity.

She had seen something before in the same area - but it turned
out to be when she was doing her paperround when she was ten!!!

morning, 30 Jan, went ourtside and found a piece of

metal on his patio and became very concerned and
thencalled the local Fire Brigade.

3. Location, indoor/outdoor, He was inside
stationary/moving

heights are unreliable)

a known landmark) quarters of mile

may be of more use than
estimates of course and speed)

observations (Moving cloud,
haze, mist, etc)

26 Jan 95

FILE NOTE

1. PM on Thu 26 Jan I received a call fromElem of

Hatfield Peverell
Chelmsford

2. On Wednesday 18 Jan at 2030 hours ReaSection 40 se saw a

light moving west to east which travelled 20-50 miles in

5-7 seconds. The night was clear and there were planes in the
vicinity. The object was green/blue in colour and very large.

3. Not much to go on here! I advised BERING of our interest
in such matters, and that if a possible explanation were
forthcoming I would let him know.
